Frontend uses `planReviewData.wait_seconds` as the countdown source when present, falling back to `roundWaitSeconds`. Previously the plan review countdown always used `roundWaitSeconds`, which diverged from the backend timeout when `auto_approve_plans` was enabled with a different `plan_review_wait_seconds`.
  - **ResultsPanel integration summary (`frontend/src/components/ResultsPanel.tsx`)**: Integration report summary rendered in the ResultsPanel round card (matching the existing display in AnalysisPanel), providing consistent UX across both UI panels.

- `0.32.6` — Integration round fixes: WS message type registration, PI_DECIDE routing bypass, round serialization, phase-qualified keys, report summary surface (2026-07-05):
  - **WS message type registration (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: `integration_gate` and `integration_complete` added to `WebSocketMessage.type` Literal. Previously missing, causing `ValidationError` outside `messaging.py`'s try/except and crashing `_integration_synthesis` under `@with_retry`, overrunning `integration_cycle`. New guard test (`test_websocket_message_types.py`) validates every `_send_message` type in workflow/ is registered.
  - **Integration-gate PI_DECIDE routing bypass (`backend/workflow/direction_coverage.py`, `backend/workflow/routing.py`)**: New `awaiting_integration_direction()` predicate detects when auto-skip set `PI_DECIDE_SIGNAL` with `integration_cycle > 0`. `_should_continue` honors it in the integration-loop guard, so PI direction selection can reach `pi_planning` past `max_rounds`. The integration cycle cap still stops the loop in `_check_convergence_condition`. New test file (`test_integration_pi_decide_routing.py`, 3 tests).
  - **Centralized round serialization (`backend/routes/serialization.py` — new)**: `serialize_round_results()` preserves `phase`, `integration_candidates`, and `integration_report_summary` for all round results. Used by both `websockets.py` (completed-session reconnect) and `reports.py` (report-load), replacing duplicated inline serialization that dropped integration fields. New test file (`test_round_serialization.py`).
  - **Phase-qualified round keys (`frontend/src/utils/roundKey.ts` — new)**: `roundKey(phase, round)` returns `"int-N"` for integration rounds and `"r-N"` for exploration/innovation, preventing React key collisions and reload de-dup issues when integration rounds reuse the same round number as the previous innovation round. AnalysisPanel's `expandedRound` state type changed from `number` to `string`; ResultsPanel's `expandedRounds` Set changed from `Set<number>` to `Set<string>`. WS `round_summary` handler de-dups by `roundKey` instead of round number. New test file (`roundKey.test.ts`, 4 tests).
  - **Surface integration report summary + candidate scores (`frontend/src/types.ts`, `frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`, `frontend/src/utils/transformLoadResponse.ts`, `frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/ResultsPanel.tsx`)**: `integration_report_summary` mapped end-to-end (raw type → store → transform → component) and rendered in integration round cards. ResultsPanel now shows candidate scores matching AnalysisPanel. Updated WS test (`useApi.ws.test.ts`) verifies integration round stays separate from same-numbered innovation round.
  - **Design spec**: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-05-fix-integration-round-issues.md` (911 lines) documents all 5 issues, reproduction paths, and implementation steps.

- `0.32.5` — Integration gate auto-skip → PI decide, integration RoundResult, graph short-circuit for zero direction, shared round-summary payload, frontend integration round display (2026-07-05):
  - **Integration gate auto-skip now triggers PI_DECIDE (`backend/workflow/synthesis.py`)**: `_wait_for_user_input` integration-gate timeout no longer sets `final_report_requested`. Instead, it sets `user_chosen_direction = PI_DECIDE_SIGNAL` and switches phase to `INNOVATION`, giving PI a chance to select directions from integration candidates. User explicit finalize (via `/finalize-from-integration` API) continues to set `final_report_requested` directly.
  - **Integration gate accepts let_pi_decide (`backend/routes/interaction.py`)**: `_choose_direction_locked` now allows `let_pi_decide=True` at the integration gate, switching to `INNOVATION` phase instead of rejecting. Test updated: `test_integration_gate_accepts_let_pi_decide` replaces the old rejection test.
  - **Integration round RoundResult creation (`backend/workflow/integration.py`)**: `_integration_synthesis` now creates a `RoundResult` with `phase="integration"`, `integration_report_summary`, and `integration_candidates`. Round summary WebSocket messages (`round_summary`) include `phase` and `integration_candidates` fields. Integration detailed segments now pass the integration `RoundResult` properly.
  - **Graph short-circuit for zero integration directions (`backend/workflow/graph.py`, `backend/workflow/routing.py`)**: New conditional edge `_after_pi_planning` routes to `generate_final_report` when PI selects 0 integration directions (via `final_report_requested`), skipping the agent pipeline. New `_after_pi_planning()` method in `RoutingMixin`.
  - **Shared round-summary payload builder (`backend/workflow/synthesis.py`)**: `build_round_summary_payload()` extracted from `_pi_synthesis` into a standalone helper, shared between regular rounds and integration rounds. Payload now consistently includes `phase` and `integration_candidates`.
  - **Integration gate PI_DECIDE routing in pi_planning (`backend/workflow/planning.py`)**: `_pi_planning` distinguishes integration-gate PI_DECIDE (via `integration_cycle > 0`) from exploration-gate PI_DECIDE. PI selects from `integration_gate_candidates` via `select_integration_directions()`. When PI selects 0 directions, sets `final_report_requested=True` and returns empty instructions with skip_nodes to short-circuit. PI agent gains `select_integration_directions()` method.
  - **Integration gate PI selection agent method (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`)**: New `select_integration_directions()` — PI evaluates scored candidates from integration analysis and selects genuinely novel, evidence-supported directions. Unlike `select_best_directions`, it does NOT force at least 1 direction; empty selection is a valid outcome.
  - **`RoundResult` schema extended (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: `RoundResult.phase` now allows `"integration"` in addition to `"exploration"` and `"innovation"`. New fields `integration_report_summary` and `integration_candidates` store integration-phase data. `_serialize_round_results()` includes `phase`, `integration_report_summary`, and `integration_candidates`.
  - **Frontend integration round display (`frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/ResultsPanel.tsx`)**: Integration rounds render with purple styling, labeled "Integration N" ("整合轮 N"), with candidate research directions listed in a purple-themed card. `IntegrationGatePanel` gains "Let PI decide" button and countdown timer with auto-trigger. No-wait mode (`countdownSeconds=0`) triggers PI decide immediately.
  - **Frontend type plumbing (`frontend/src/types.ts`, `frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`, `frontend/src/hooks/useApi.ts`, `frontend/src/utils/transformLoadResponse.ts`)**: `RoundSummary` and `RoundResult` gain `phase` and `integrationCandidates` fields. WebSocket `round_summary` handler, store persistence, and load-response transformer all support the new fields. `GitMerge` icon imported.
  - **New test files (4 files)**: `test_integration_synthesis_node.py` (integration round result creation, round summary message). Updated `test_integration_endpoints.py` (let_pi_decide accepted at integration gate), `IntegrationGate.test.tsx` (PI decide button, countdown timer prop).
  - **New untracked test files (2)**: `test_integration_gate_autoskip.py`, `test_integration_round_result.py`.

- `0.32.4` — Plan review wait aligned with round-wait semantics (2026-07-05):
  - **Plan review wait unified with round_wait_seconds (`backend/workflow/planning.py`)**: `resolve_plan_review_wait()` now inherits `round_wait_seconds` semantics instead of always returning `-1` when `auto_approve_plans` is `False`. Plan review now follows the same auto-skip (0) / countdown (>0) / manual (-1) behavior as all other HITL gates. Timeout while waiting for plan review action now always auto-skips instead of sending a status message, matching the round-wait pattern.
  - **Tests restructured (`backend/tests/test_plan_review_wait.py`)**: 6 focused tests replace 4 ambiguous ones — each test case covers one unambiguous semantic: round_wait=0 auto-skips, positive round_wait applies to plan review, manual (-1) blocks plan review, auto_approve_plans uses dedicated plan_review_wait_seconds, auto_approve + zero seconds auto-skips.

- `0.32.3` — Code deduplication refactoring: prompt blocks, gate predicates, WS payload builders & frontend selection reset (2026-07-04):
  - **Shared prompt-fragment builder (`backend/agents/prompt_blocks.py`)**: New `build_integration_block()` with three styles (`final`/`integrated`/`detailed`) replaces 3× duplicated integration-reports inlining across PI and Supervisor agents. PI prompts (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`) and Supervisor report prompt (`backend/agents/supervisor_agent.py`) both consume it via `from .prompt_blocks import build_integration_block`.
  - **Shared download-section HTML renderer (`backend/services/report/download_section.py`)**: New `render_related_reports_downloads_html()` provides a single renderer for the "Related Reports & Downloads" appendix block, consumed by SupervisorAgent appendix (`backend/agents/supervisor_agent.py`), report regenerator (`backend/services/report/regenerator.py`), and report sections (`backend/services/report/sections.py`).
  - **Shared direction-record entry formula (`backend/services/report/interaction_records.py`)**: Extracted `_direction_record_entry_pairs()` and `direction_record_entries()` as single-source-of-truth for direction-record filename/label computation. Both `write_interaction_record_files()` and the downloads renderer consume the same formula. Early-exit guard prevents `mkdir` when nothing to write.
  - **Shared capture helpers (`backend/services/interaction_record_capture.py`)**: New `populate_direction_record_from_state()` and `resolve_integration_gate()` deduplicate direction-record construction across `_choose_direction_locked` and `finalize_from_integration` route handlers (`backend/routes/interaction.py`). `resolve_integration_gate()` also clears `integration_gate_candidates`/`literature_evidence` as a single atomic operation.
  - **Shared integration-gate payload builder (`backend/workflow/helpers.py`)**: `build_integration_gate_payload()` replaces 3× inlined payload construction across `core.py` (reconnect send), `integration.py`, and `snapshots.py` (snapshot restore re-send). Consumed via `from .helpers import build_integration_gate_payload`.
  - **Shared integration-gate predicates (`backend/workflow/direction_coverage.py`)**: `at_integration_gate()`, `active_batch_incomplete()`, `active_batch_complete()` replace duplicated batch-coverage and phase+candidate checks across `core.py`, `routing.py`, `synthesis.py`, `reporting.py`, and `websockets.py`. `_multi_direction_analysis_completed()` uses `active_batch_complete()`. `validate_complete_report_allowed()` uses `active_batch_incomplete()`.
  - **Gate string constants (`backend/constants.py`)**: New `GATE_ROUND_WAIT`, `GATE_DIRECTION_SELECTION`, `GATE_INTEGRATION_SELECTION`, `GATE_ANNOTATION_CONFIRMATION`, and `SEMANTIC_WAIT_GATES` frozenset replace raw string literals across `core.py`, `synthesis.py`, `lifecycle.py`, `restore_targets.py`. `SNAPSHOT_RESUME_TARGET` in `restore_targets.py` uses the constants.
  - **`consume_final_report_request()` method (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: `AnalysisState.consume_final_report_request()` provides an atomic read-and-clear of the one-shot `final_report_requested` flag, replacing manual `=False` assignments in `core.py` (report-only reconnect path) and `synthesis.py` (integration finalize path).
  - **`_check_convergence_condition` simplification (`backend/workflow/routing.py`)**: Uses shared predicates from `direction_coverage.py` for all batch/coverage checks. Eliminated duplicated `max_rounds` fallback logic — now delegates to `_should_continue()` via `return self._should_continue(state)` instead of reimplementing max-rounds checking.
  - **`_in_integration_loop` WS guard optimization (`backend/routes/websockets.py`)**: Cheap boolean early-return checks (`pending_integration`, `phase == INTEGRATION`, `integration_gate_candidates`) before O(N) batch scan via `active_batch_incomplete()`.
  - **Frontend selection reset (`frontend/src/components/IntegrationGatePanel.tsx`)**: `useEffect` resets checkbox state when `cycle` or `candidates` changes, preventing stale selections from a previous cycle from leaking into a new candidate set.
   - **10 new test files** (190+ lines): `test_prompt_blocks.py`, `test_download_section.py`, `test_gate_constants.py`, `test_integration_gate_payload.py`, `test_integration_predicates.py`, `test_consume_final_report_request.py`, `test_resolve_integration_gate.py`, `test_write_records_guard.py`, `IntegrationGate.test.tsx` (selection reset). Updated 5 existing tests to assert against new helper names instead of raw inlined code.

- `0.32.2` — User-interaction capture records & report integration (2026-07-04):
  - **New models `SelectionEntry`, `DirectionSelectionRecord`, `AnnotationClusterEntry`, `AnnotationRecord` (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: Immutable snapshot models for direction-selection events (exploration, integration-gate, PI-decide, finalize) and annotation-confirmation events. State fields: `direction_selection_records`, `annotation_record`, `annotation_supplementary_requests`, `integration_gate_literature_evidence`.
  - **New capture service (`backend/services/interaction_record_capture.py`)**: Pure helper functions `get_or_open_direction_record()`, `find_open_pi_decide_record()`, `map_selections()` (with `is_custom` inference), `build_annotation_record()`. No I/O or LLM calls.
  - **Direction record wiring (`backend/routes/interaction.py`)**: `_choose_direction_locked()` captures candidates, literature evidence, and selections (including `is_custom` flag) into `DirectionSelectionRecord`. Integration-gate finalize via `finalize_from_integration()` captures the same with `event_type = "finalize"`. Early validation rejects missing direction choice before opening a record.
  - **Annotation record wiring (`backend/routes/interaction.py`, `backend/workflow/annotation.py`)**: Both manual confirmation and auto-confirm/timeout paths call `build_annotation_record()`. Auto-confirm path also captures supplementary requests.
  - **PI-decide record wiring (`backend/workflow/planning.py`)**: PI autonomous direction selection (`select_best_directions` path) creates `DirectionSelectionRecord` with `event_type = "pi_decide"` via `get_or_open_direction_record()`.
  - **Supplementary request capture (`backend/workflow/synthesis.py`, `backend/workflow/annotation.py`)**: Supplementary requests during direction selection and annotation gates appended to the open record.
  - **Integration gate literature evidence persistence (`backend/workflow/integration.py`, `backend/routes/interaction.py`)**: `integration_gate_literature_evidence` state field preserves literature evidence across integration cycles. Cleared on direction selection or finalize alongside `integration_gate_candidates`.
  - **Standalone HTML renderers (`backend/services/report/interaction_records.py`)**: `render_direction_record_html()` and `render_annotation_record_html()` produce standalone HTML files (embedded CSS, no JS). `write_interaction_record_files()` writes them to the reports directory. Renders: selected/unselected candidate cards, literature evidence, custom direction tags, PI-decide banner, supporting data, annotation tables with proportion columns, original-vs-final cell type display, supplementary requests.
  - **Supervisor appendix migration (`backend/agents/supervisor_agent.py`)**: `_build_appendix()` calls `write_interaction_record_files()` and replaces hardcoded `"exploration_report.html"` / `"annotation_context_report.html"` links with dynamically generated records.
  - **Generator, regenerator & sections sync (`backend/services/report/generator.py`, `backend/services/report/regenerator.py`, `backend/services/report/sections.py`)**: All three updated to use new record files instead of hardcoded exploration/annotation report links. Sections downloads use conditional existence checks.
  - **Frontend `is_custom` plumbing (`frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`)**: New `buildDirectionsPayload()` helper maps direction objects (including `isCustom`) to API format. Integration-gate and exploration direction selection both use it. New test in `AnalysisPanel.isCustom.test.tsx`.
  - **API model update (`backend/models/api_requests.py`)**: `DirectionChoice.directions` type relaxed from `List[Dict[str, str]]` to `List[Dict[str, Any]]` to support the `is_custom` field.
   - **12 new test files**: 7 capture unit tests, 7 wiring tests (including supervisor appendix integration, record writer output dir isolation), 4 model default tests, 8 render tests, 2 integration endpoint tests (literature evidence clearing), 1 frontend test, and `test_to_api_dict_coverage.py` exclusion updates.

- `0.32.1` — Integration round loop (cross-direction synthesis + new-direction gate):
  - **New graph node `integration_synthesis` (`backend/workflow/integration.py`)**: Independent integration round orchestrating PI cumulative synthesis + Literature open search + PI candidate generation + Literature novelty gate + PI scoring. Skipped bioinformatics/pathology/biochemistry agents. Three-way routing: `gate` → `wait_for_user_input`, `stop` → `generate_final_report`. Integration analysis is cumulative across all innovation rounds (round 2+).
  - **New `AnalysisPhase.INTEGRATION` + state fields (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: `integration_cycle`, `max_integration_cycles` (default 3), `pending_integration`, `active_direction_batch`, `last_integrated_round`, `integration_gate_candidates`, `integration_reports`, `final_report_requested`.
  - **Batch-aware direction coverage (`backend/workflow/direction_coverage.py`)**: `direction_coverage_status_for_batch()` replaces fragile round-count + `directions_queue` completion checks with explicit batch comparison.
  - **Three-way convergence routing (`backend/workflow/routing.py`)**: `check_convergence` expanded from `{continue, stop}` to `{continue, integrate, stop}`. `_should_continue` hard-stop guard bypassed when `pending_integration` is set, batch is incomplete, or at integration gate.
  - **Integration gate in synthesis (`backend/workflow/synthesis.py`)**: Removed inline `_should_run_direction_correlation` (moved to integration node). `_pi_synthesis` sets `pending_integration` on batch complete. `_wait_for_user_input` handles integration-gate wait state with finalize/new-direction two-way exit.
  - **Lifecycle + snapshot restore (`backend/routes/snapshots.py`, `backend/routes/lifecycle.py`, `backend/workflow/restore_targets.py`)**: `pre_integration_gate` snapshot phase with `integration_selection` resume gate. Phase-aware status restored as `WAITING_INPUT`. Realtime `integration_gate` message re-sent on restore.
  - **`/finalize-from-integration` endpoint (`backend/routes/interaction.py`)**: Dedicated API for user to exit integration gate and request final report. `_choose_direction` integration-gate branch sets batch + phase = INNOVATION.
  - **WebSocket report-only guard (`backend/routes/websockets.py`)**: `_in_integration_loop()` helper prevents sessions in integration loop from taking the `needs_report_only` shortcut.
  - **Complete report gate (`backend/workflow/reporting.py`)**: `validate_complete_report_allowed` checks uncovered integration batch; blocks complete report until batch is done.
  - **PI + Literature agent methods (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`, `backend/agents/literature_agent.py`)**: `generate_integration_direction_candidates`, `score_integration_directions_with_evidence`, `integration_literature_search`, `evaluate_direction_novelty_candidates`. Supervisor detailed report includes integration_reports.
  - **Frontend integration gate panel (`frontend/src/components/IntegrationGatePanel.tsx`)**: Minimal candidate checklist + submit/finalize actions. `AnalysisPanel.tsx` listens for `integration_gate` messages, wires `apiFetch` to `/choose-direction` and `/finalize-from-integration`. New `integration_gate`/`integration_complete` message types in `appStore.ts`.
   - **17 new test files** covering batch coverage, convergence routing, direction candidates, endpoints, gate wait, graph wiring, literature search, report gate, restore target, resume gate, round state, routing, snapshot route, synthesis node, WS guard, pending integration flag, and PI-decide batch setting. Removed obsolete `test_multi_direction_correlation_trigger.py`.

**0.31.x — (2026-07-02)**

- `0.31.6` — Annotation confirmation cell proportion column (2026-07-03):
  - **New proportion computation helper (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: `compute_cluster_group_proportions()` computes per-cluster cell proportions per experimental group via sandbox `pd.crosstab`. `_select_proportion_grouping()` picks the `condition_like` column that best maps to `state.treatment_groups` (case/whitespace-insensitive), falling back to overall ("整体") when no column matches. `_ordered_proportion_labels()` sorts labels by treatment group `order` then alphabetically. `_normalize_group_label()`, `_matching_treatment_labels()` extracted for reusable matching logic.
  - **New schema fields (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: `ClusterAnnotation.group_proportions: Dict[str, float]` stores per-cluster proportion values. `AnnotationConfirmationState.proportion_group_labels` and `.proportion_dimension` carry the column-level metadata.
  - **Workflow integration (`backend/workflow/annotation.py`)**: `_annotate_clusters_callback()` calls `compute_cluster_group_proportions()` before building `AnnotationConfirmationState`, both in the initial annotation pass and the supplementary round. New `_apply_group_proportions()` helper maps proportion data onto `ClusterAnnotation` objects. `_build_annotation_confirmation_payload()` includes `proportion_group_labels` and `proportion_dimension` for frontend consumption.
  - **Frontend proportion column (`frontend/src/components/AnnotationConfirmationDialog.tsx`)**: New proportion table column with dynamic header ("Cell Proportion (Control/NASH)", "细胞比例", etc.) based on `proportionDimension` and labels. `renderProportions()` displays per-cluster values as `"12.5% / 8.0%"` or `"—"` when unavailable. `annotationKey` dependency includes proportion labels/dimension so the table refreshes on proportion data changes.
  - **Frontend prop plumbing (`frontend/src/components/AnalysisPanel.tsx`)**: Passes `proportion_group_labels` and `proportion_dimension` from `AnnotationData` to the dialog.
  - **Spec update (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-03-annotation-cell-proportion-column-design.md`)**: Tightened selection rules — partial condition-like matches now fall back to overall instead of using partial labels; `pd.crosstab` result columns not in pre-sorted labels are appended alphabetically to avoid silent drops when experimental groups >5.
  - **New/updated test files (6 files)**: `test_annotation_validation_schema.py` (5 tests: proportion defaults, mapping, preservation), `test_annotation_validation_introspect.py` (1 test: `group`-named condition column detection), `test_annotation_validation_callback_behavior.py` (1 test: callback wires proportion computation before confirmation state), `AnnotationConfirmationDialog.validation.test.tsx` (4 tests: proportion header/values, overall dimension, empty labels, refresh on proportion change).

- `0.31.5` — Unauthenticated health endpoint, login/register input trimming (2026-07-03):
  - **New `/api/health` endpoint (`backend/routes/root.py`)**: Unauthenticated liveness probe returning `{"status": "ok", "version": VERSION}`. Replaces `/api/config/status` for Docker HEALTHCHECK and entrypoint readiness — unlike the config endpoint, `/api/health` stays public after admin setup.
  - **Health endpoint made public (`backend/middleware/auth.py`)**: `/api/health` added to `PUBLIC_EXACT` paths so container orchestrators and entrypoint probes never hit auth.
  - **Docker HEALTHCHECK & entrypoint probe (`Dockerfile`, `docker-entrypoint.sh`)**: Both changed from `curl ... /api/config/status` to `curl ... /api/health`, ensuring health checks work consistently across all deployment stages.
  - **Login/Register input trimming (`frontend/src/pages/Login.tsx`, `frontend/src/pages/Register.tsx`)**: Username field trimmed via `username.trim()` before submission, preventing accidental whitespace from causing login/registration failures.

- `0.31.5.fix` — Config annotation_validation_timeout_seconds default fix (2026-07-03):
  - **Annotation validation timeout default (`backend/config/_config.py`)**: `annotation_validation_timeout_seconds` default corrected from 180 to 600 to match the intended production value.

- `0.31.4` — Docker image size reduction, Results figure discovery extraction & bounded brief-gallery fallback (2026-07-02):
  - **Docker image size reduction (`Dockerfile`, `.dockerignore`)**: Removed build-time `pip install -r requirements.txt` layer (previously added ~7 GB). `dep/` added to `.dockerignore`. The runtime entrypoint continues to install dependencies into `/dep/venv` at startup, restoring the pre-0.31.1 small-image contract (~1 GB).
  - **New `figure_discovery.py` helper (`backend/services/report/figure_discovery.py`)**: Workspace figure scanning extracted into a standalone, testable module. Deduplicates by stem with source priority ordering (`figures/` > workspace root > `*_outputs/figures`). Path traversal protection via `validate_path_within`; symlink PDFs outside workspace dropped. Generic type inference only — no hardcoded species/tissue/disease names.
  - **`_assemble_brief_figures` extracted (`backend/workflow/reporting.py`)**: Inlined figure assembly logic refactored into `_assemble_brief_figures()` method. Uses `discover_workspace_figures()` with 200-candidate cap for PI selection. Defensive name validation strips hallucinated PI selections, preventing empty Results gallery — falls back to publication figures when all names are invalid. `is_publication` flag added to selection input for fallback priority.
  - **Bounded LLM-failure fallback (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`)**: `select_brief_figures` no longer dumps every candidate on failure. New `_bounded_brief_fallback()` returns only confident "good" candidates: publication figures, then result-type figures (umap/volcano/dotplot/heatmap/barplot) excluding QC diagnostics. Returns empty list when only QC/ambiguous figures exist, letting the reporting layer publication fallback take over. `safe_parse_json_array` replaces raw `json.loads` for robustness.
  - **New test files (3 files, 425+ lines)**: `test_figure_discovery.py` (13 unit tests), `test_reporting_figure_discovery.py` (3 integration tests: cap, LLM-failure bounding, invalid-name self-heal), `test_select_brief_figures_fallback.py` (10 tests: fallback logic + PI-agent integration).

- `0.31.3` — Empty report content validation, empty-response retry & report model re-resolution on retry (2026-07-02):
  - **Empty report content validation (`backend/agents/supervisor_agent.py`)**: `_validate_report_content()` prevents saving empty/placeholder content as a completed report. Raises `UpstreamExhaustedError` when the model returns zero-token empty content after retries, driving the workflow HITL loop for Retry / change-model / Skip.
  - **Empty zero-token response retry (`backend/utils/llm_client.py`)**: Both `_chat_inline` and `_invoke_adapter` now detect empty responses with `completion_tokens == 0` and retry up to 3× with backoff instead of surfacing a bare `""`. New `EmptyResponseError` for the protocol-probe path (classified transient so the probe retries the same protocol). `UpstreamExhaustedError` raised on exhaustion. `EMPTY_RESPONSE_MAX_RETRIES = 3`.
  - **Report model re-resolution per retry attempt (`backend/routes/reports.py`, `backend/workflow/reporting.py`)**: `pi_model` and `bio_model` resolved inside the retry loop so changing the report model in session settings takes effect on retry. TERMINATE action in detailed report generation now properly raises `WorkflowTerminatedError`.
  - **New test files**: `test_report_empty_validation.py` (2 tests), expanded `test_truncation_retry.py` (+120 lines, 8 new tests for zero-token retry, adapter empty retry, probe path).

- `0.31.2` — Pathology modality-boundary warning precision, alignment with modality data model (2026-07-02):
  - **Pathology boundary warning precision (`backend/workflow/synthesis.py`)**: `build_disabled_pathology_warning` now checks for `section == "pathology"` (data_files) or `type == "pathology"` (pending uploads) instead of broadly matching image extensions and data types. This eliminates false-positive warnings when non-pathology images (e.g., biochemistry panel PNGs, generic PI-chat uploads) are present while pathology is disabled.
  - **Tests refined (`backend/tests/test_agent_modality_boundaries.py`)**: Restructured the test suite to add dedicated cases for pathology-section images, pending pathology uploads, pending chat images, and non-pathology inputs, aligning with the modality data model.
- `0.31.1` — LLM failure per-node HITL, sandbox PKL integrity, upload concurrency hardening, API key security, frontend ErrorBoundary & WS reliability (2026-07-02):
  - **LLM failure generation per-node (`backend/workflow/core.py`, `backend/workflow/state.py`)**: Changed from a single global generation counter to per-node counters (`_llm_failure_generations: Dict[str, int]`), preventing stale/overlapping generation checks when multiple nodes fail concurrently. Added `_next_generation(node)` and `_generation_for(node)` helpers.
  - **WebSocket race condition fixes (`frontend/src/hooks/useApi.ts`, `frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`)**: Added stale connection guard (`wsRef.current !== ws`) in both `onopen` and `onclose` handlers. Changed reconnect strategy to close existing connections before opening new ones. Switched message handlers from closure-captured `setMessages`/`addMessage`/`updateSession` to `useAppStore.getState()` direct calls, eliminating stale-state bugs on reconnect.
  - **Upload concurrency & bounds hardening (`backend/services/upload.py`)**: Added reference-counted `complete_lock` registry to prevent memory leaks from abandoned upload sessions. Added `total_size` validation at upload init (rejects >10 GB) and `chunk_index` bounds checking. Added `_stream_generic_upload()` with 100 MB size cap and chunked streaming for non-sequencing files.
  - **Sandbox PKL integrity & per-session temp dirs (`backend/utils/sandbox.py`)**: HMAC-SHA256 signatures for session pickle files, stored outside the session directory. `_verify_pkl_integrity` prevents restore of tampered payloads. Per-session temp directories (`scai-{session_id}`) replace shared `/tmp` access. Added `await_pending_saves()` before kernel shutdown to prevent save races.
  - **API key security (`frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`, `backend/routes/config.py`, `backend/services/config_io.py`, `backend/utils/settings_provider.py`)**: `deepResearchApiKey` and `googleApiKey` stripped from localStorage persistence. `apiConfig.apiKey` always stored as empty string in persisted state, with server-side validation via new `/api/config/validate-saved`. Literature config and agent-server-config GET endpoints now mask API keys (`***`). New `AgentSettingsProvider.mask_literature_config()` method. Password `hash_password` raises `ValueError` for >72 bytes (bcrypt limit).
  - **LLM client hardening (`backend/utils/llm_client.py`, `backend/utils/llm_logger.py`)**: Both `OpenAICompatibleClient` and `GoogleDeepResearchClient` have `_closed` guards preventing double-free of HTTP connection pools. `__del__` warns on leaked clients. Async LLM logging via `log_response_async()` with serialized thread-pool offloading under `asyncio.Lock()`. API key validated non-empty on construction. Idempotent close guards prevent double-free.
  - **Workflow runner & report atomicity (`backend/services/workflow_runner.py`, `backend/services/report/regenerator.py`)**: Extracted `_build_workflow_for_session()` for shared workflow construction. Report generation tracked with `_report_tasks` dict and `done_callback` cleanup. Added `report_ws_lock` (`asyncio.Lock`) around `report_websockets` read/write. `_try_begin_generation()` with `_pending_lock` for atomic check-and-set on report generation. NaN-safe JSON parsing via `_load_report_json()` helper.
  - **Session config & mutation safety (`backend/services/config_io.py`, `backend/workflow/direction_coverage.py`)**: Temperature stored as `float` instead of `str`. `normalize_legacy_round_result` returns deep copy, preventing mutation of original `RoundResult` objects. Added `count_innovation_rounds_done()` for accurate innovation-round counting; synthesis phase transition uses it instead of naive round counting.
  - **Docker & infrastructure hardening (`Dockerfile`, `docker-entrypoint.sh`, `.dockerignore`, `stop.sh`)**: Non-root user (`scai:scai`, UID 10001) with `gosu` privilege drop. `HEALTHCHECK` added. More restrictive `.dockerignore`. `stop.sh` uses `grep`/`cut` instead of `source` for PID file parsing.
  - **Security hardening (`backend/middleware/auth.py`, `backend/models/user.py`, `backend/routes/sessions.py`, `scripts/fetch_markers.py`)**: File path traversal prevention in session file operations. Session ID validation in lifecycle endpoints. Password `PasswordStr` model enforces 72-byte limit. CellMarker2 download uses `https://` with SHA-256 signature pinning. Stale lock reclaim uses atomic `os.replace()`.
  - **Frontend reliability (`frontend/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/Sidebar.tsx`, `frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`, `frontend/src/types.ts`)**: New `ErrorBoundary` component wraps full app. Sidebar defaults moved to i18n keys. `Message.content` typed as `string | ContentBlock`. Chat message `id` made required with `crypto.randomUUID()` auto-generation. New `apiConfigured` store flag for API key state checks.
  - **Admin & lifecycle fixes (`backend/routes/admin.py`, `backend/routes/lifecycle.py`, `backend/routes/reports.py`)**: `invalidate_admin_initialized_cache()` on setup to prevent stale admin state. `demote_admin_if_not_last()` prevents removing the last admin. Pause preserves plan review state across pause/resume. Auto-snapshot `WorkflowTerminatedError` now properly re-raised. Session loading `pop("report_blocked", None)` to avoid stale blocked-report state.
  - **50+ new test files**: Coverage for blob store digests, config masking/validation, fetch_markers lock atomicity, HITL generation per-node, innovation rounds counting, sandbox dangerous patterns/write-boundary/PKL integrity, upload chunk/generic bounds, normalization immutability, password bounds, report blocked/NaN/pending-lock/subdir-files/WS-registry/partial-recovery, save state task ref, session ID validation, technique agent escaping, tool dispatch resilience, version consistency, and more.

**0.30.x — (2026-06-30 → 2026-07-01)**

- `0.30.6` — Modality warning & report-blocked WebSocket message types (2026-07-01):
  - **New WebSocket message types (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: `modality_warning` and `report_blocked` added to `WebSocketMessage` allowed message types, enabling the backend to surface modality-related warnings (e.g., imaging data uploaded but pathology agent disabled) and report-generation blocks (e.g., insufficient direction coverage) as structured messages.
  - **Frontend modality/report-block display (`frontend/src/components/MessageStream.tsx`, `frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`)**: `modality_warning` renders as an amber-themed warning with `AlertTriangle` icon and modal header. `report_blocked` renders as a report-generation-blocked card with reason and partial-approval status. Both added to the `Message` type union in `appStore.ts`.

- `0.30.5` — Snapshot size policy, branch lineage restore, direction coverage tracking, complete-report validation gate, sandbox subprocess hardening, LLM retry-propagation fix (2026-07-01):
  - **Snapshot size governance (`backend/config/_config.py`, `backend/utils/snapshot.py`)**: New `MAX_SNAPSHOT_KERNEL_PKL_BYTES` (2 GB) and `MAX_SESSION_SNAPSHOT_BYTES` (20 GB) caps prevent oversized snapshots from silently consuming disk space. `SNAPSHOT_EXCLUDED_WORKSPACE_PATTERNS` filter out transient files (`core.*`, `*.tmp`, `*.lock`). Snapshot manager now conditionally includes runtime state (compressed `.pkl.zst`) and tracks skipped files with reasons. `Snapshot` model gains `runtime_state_included`, `skipped_files`, `branch_id`.
  - **Branch lineage on snapshot restore (`backend/routes/snapshots.py`, `backend/workflow/restore_targets.py`, `backend/workflow/core.py`, `backend/utils/snapshot.py`)**: Restoring a snapshot creates a branch lineage (e.g., `branch-<snapshot_id>`) instead of mutating main-line round results, preventing restored analysis from silently overwriting main-line artifacts. `SNAPSHOT_RESUME_TARGET` maps snapshot phases to resume targets (`semantic_gate` / `graph_node` / `terminal`). `resume_restored_wait_state()` handles continuation from wait-state snapshots. `AnalysisState` gains `restore_resume_*` and `restore_branch_*` fields.
  - **Direction coverage tracking (`backend/workflow/direction_coverage.py`)**: New `DirectionCoverageStatus` tracks which selected innovation directions have completed substantive evidence rounds. `current_lineage_id()`, `normalize_direction_key()`, `completed_innovation_direction_keys()`, `has_substantive_findings()` replace fragile round-count comparisons. `clone_round_results_for_branch()` creates branch lineage views for snapshot restore and report isolation. `RoundResult` gains `lineage_id`, `branch_id`, `branch_label`, `source_snapshot_id`, `restored_from_snapshot_id`, `direction_key`, `result_identity_key`, `round_status`, `evidence_status` fields.
  - **Round completion validation (`backend/workflow/synthesis.py`)**: `derive_round_completion_status()` maps actual agent execution state (core/optional failures, partials, skipped) to canonical `round_status` (completed/partial/failed/skipped) and `evidence_status` (substantive/empty/failed). `_upsert_round_result()` uses `result_identity_key` for idempotent dedup. `_build_directions_with_results_for_correlation()` reads from `direction_coverage` rather than reverse-matching innovation round count.
  - **Complete-report validation gate (`backend/workflow/reporting.py`, `backend/workflow/graph.py`, `backend/routes/reports.py`)**: `validate_complete_report_allowed()` blocks final report when selected innovation directions have missing completed evidence. `generate_final_report` node has a conditional edge routing blocked reports directly to END. User override via `POST /.../report/partial/approve` sets `allow_partial_report=True`. `AnalysisState` gains `allow_partial_report`, `partial_report_requested_at`.
  - **Report branch isolation (`backend/services/report/paths.py`, `backend/services/report/lineage.py`, `backend/services/report/generator.py`, `backend/routes/reports.py`)**: Report generation uses `build_lineage_report_state()` to filter `round_results` to the target lineage. All report endpoints (HTML, JSON, figures, tables, ZIP download) accept `branch_id` query parameter and resolve output directories via `resolve_report_output_dir()`. Report memory cache keyed by `session_id:lineage_id`. `clear_detailed_report_cache()` clears all lineage caches on session deletion.
  - **Sandbox subprocess hardening (`backend/utils/sandbox.py`)**: `_is_safe_os_system()` now always returns false — no `os.system` calls are allowed in notebook execution. `_is_safe_subprocess()` blocks ALL subprocess execution calls (`run`, `call`, `check_call`, `check_output`, `Popen`). AST validation gains alias propagation tracking — catches re-assignment patterns (`sp = subprocess; sp.run(...)`) and `from subprocess import run` imports. `subprocess` and `os` modules are fully blocked for code execution.
  - **Bioinformatics agent pip-install removal (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: MoE prompt replaces "use subprocess to pip install missing packages" with "report the missing dependency, explain which analysis step is limited, and use an already-installed alternative." ModuleNotFoundError hint text updated to match — no longer extracts package name and suggests pip install command.
  - **LLM retry-propagation fix (`backend/utils/llm_client.py`)**: `non_retryable_predicate` and `max_retries_override` now properly forwarded through lambda closures in protocol dispatch — probe-fallback and inline calls no longer ignore per-agent retry configuration. `invoke_inline()` wrapper ensures parameters are captured at call time.
  - **Plan scope and review improvements (`backend/workflow/planning.py`)**: `populate_plan_scope()` enriches plans with `round_number`, `direction`, `direction_key`, `branch_id`, `created_from_snapshot_id`. `resolve_plan_review_wait()` supports `auto_approve_plans` and `plan_review_wait_seconds`. `approved_plan_matches_current_context()` validates plan freshness (round, direction, branch all match); stale approved plans trigger regeneration. Review wait respects auto-approve flag — when auto-approve is on, timeout triggers approval; when off, timeout sends a status message rather than skipping. `AnalysisPlan` model gains round/direction/branch scope fields; `AnalysisState` gains `plan_review_wait_seconds`, `auto_approve_plans`.
  - **Plan revision signal refactor (`backend/workflow/callbacks.py`)**: Removed fragile `PLAN_REVISION_KEYWORDS` substring-matching (which falsely triggered on e.g., "cannot execute this step because X"). Replaced with `parse_plan_revision_signal()` that checks `[__PLAN_REVISION__]` tag first, then falls back to JSON `plan_revision_needed` field. Removed brittleness TODO comment and keyword frozenset from `constants.py`.
  - **Modality boundary enforcement (`backend/agents/biochemistry_agent.py`, `backend/workflow/synthesis.py`)**: Biochemistry prompt explicitly states images represent biochemistry experimental data (blots, plates, gels), not pathology slides — no mislabeling as pathology findings. `build_disabled_pathology_warning()` emits a modality warning when image files exist but pathology agent is disabled.
  - **Workflow runner resumption fix (`backend/services/workflow_runner.py`)**: Headless workflow runner now distinguishes fresh runs from resumed sessions — calls `workflow.resume()` for existing state (`current_round > 0` or paused/waiting status) instead of always starting a fresh `workflow.run()`.
  - **Route & lifecycle improvements (`backend/routes/reports.py`, `backend/routes/sessions.py`, `backend/routes/lifecycle.py`, `backend/routes/v1.py`)**: Detailed report cache cleaned on session/analysis deletion. `approve_partial_report` endpoint added. Restored wait-state snapshots skip WS restart. Biochemistry analysis added 6th requirement for modality scope.
  - **26 new test files**: `test_direction_coverage.py`, `test_round_completion_status.py`, `test_round_result_lineage.py`, `test_report_branch_isolation.py`, `test_report_validation.py`, `test_plan_review_wait.py`, `test_plan_revision_signal.py`, `test_plan_scope.py`, `test_snapshot_restore_resume.py`, `test_snapshot_size_policy.py`, `test_workflow_runner.py`, `test_sandbox_dependency_policy.py`, `test_bioinformatics_dependency_policy.py`, `test_agent_modality_boundaries.py`, `test_llm_protocol_probe_retry.py`. Extended `test_multi_direction_correlation_trigger.py`, `test_multi_direction_terminal_gate.py`, `test_workflow_routing.py`, `test_user_review_gate_action_leak.py`, `test_to_api_dict_coverage.py`, `test_client_protocol_dispatch.py`.

- `0.30.4` — Multi-direction terminal gate stale-queue fix & snapshot restore direction resume (2026-07-01):
  - **Stale queue handling in terminal rounds (`backend/workflow/synthesis.py`)**: New `_multi_direction_analysis_completed()` helper detects completion of all selected innovation directions by comparing innovation round count against chosen direction count (not by checking if `directions_queue` is empty). P1-1 peek-commit leaves the final direction in `directions_queue` on terminal rounds because `_initialize_round()` is never called again, so the old empty-queue criterion falsely suppressed further round processing. `_should_force_next_round()` now skips stale queue entries after completion is detected, preventing infinite loops.
  - **Snapshot restore direction guard (`backend/workflow/initialize.py`)**: Direction queue consumption and `current_round_direction` clear now both guarded by `last_restored_snapshot_id` — on snapshot restore, the direction queue state is preserved exactly as it was when the snapshot was taken, preventing premature consumption of queue entries that would cause direction mismatch during replay.
  - **New test files**: `test_multi_direction_terminal_gate.py` (terminal round HITL gate test), `test_snapshot_restore_direction_resume.py` (snapshot restore direction state preservation), `test_simplify_refactor.py` (code simplification refactoring validation). Extended `test_multi_direction_correlation_trigger.py` with 5 new terminal-state tests.

- `0.30.3` — PI prompt mechanism program display refactor (2026-06-30):
  - **PI prompt compact display (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`)**: Replaced verbose inline f-string formatting of `DataDrivenMechanismProgram` fields (label, round_number, evidence_strength, specificity, bulk_support, triggering_observations, supporting_genes/pathways, missing_evidence) with a dedicated `to_prompt_text(style='compact', ...)` method call, reducing code duplication and improving maintainability.

- `0.30.2` — Data-driven mechanism program capture & report integration (2026-06-30):
  - **New tool `register_mechanism_programs` (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: Bioinformatics agent can now register data-driven mechanism programs via a new function-calling tool. `validate_mechanism_program_payload()` enforces conservative validation: requires required fields, bounds programs to 8, genes to 50, observations to 500 chars, fills defaults for alternative_explanations and missing_evidence, detects overclaiming wording in interpretations. Tool schema is mechanism-agnostic — no hardcoded mechanism names.
  - **Mandatory Gate M (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: Exploration Stage-2 downstream analysis now has a bounded (2-rejection) mandatory gate requiring `register_mechanism_programs` before `report_findings`. An empty programs list with a `no_program_reason` satisfies the gate. After exhaustion, gate is skipped with a documented note. Scoped to `analysis_phase == exploration AND planning_stage != "stage1"`.
  - **New model `DataDrivenMechanismProgram` (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: Pydantic model with fields: label, description, cell_types, conditions_compared, triggering_observations, supporting_genes/pathways/scores/tables, bulk_support, evidence_strength, specificity, alternative_explanations, missing_evidence, interpretation, downstream_literature_queries, timestamp. `RoundResult` gains `data_driven_mechanism_programs`. `AnalysisState` gains `data_driven_mechanism_programs` as cross-round accumulator. Excluded from `to_api_dict()`.
  - **Instruction injection (`backend/workflow/agents.py`)**: `_MECHANISM_CAPTURE_INSTRUCTION` injected into exploration and stage-2 instructions. Agents are instructed to derive labels from data only (no preset categories), include alternative explanations and missing evidence, and call `register_mechanism_programs` before `report_findings`.
  - **Round-result persistence (`backend/workflow/synthesis.py`)**: `round_result.data_driven_mechanism_programs` is populated from `bioinformatics_result.mechanism_programs` during round synthesis.
  - **PI prompt consumption (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`)**: Three PI prompts now consume mechanism programs: `generate_raw_direction_candidates()` (shows last 8 programs with cells/strength/specificity/observations/genes), `generate_final_summary()` (last 3 rounds, classifies as covered/integrated/uncovered/future), `generate_integrated_report()` (last 3 rounds, tags moderate/strong as `candidate mechanism` vs `exploratory signal`).
  - **Supervisor report prompt (`backend/agents/supervisor_agent.py`)**: `_build_report_prompt()` includes data-driven mechanism programs and cross-modal synthesis candidates from last 3 rounds, plus a new `[mechanism_candidates]` report section.
  - **Literature mechanism context (`backend/workflow/agents.py`)**: `_build_observed_context_for_literature()` injects mechanism programs at the front of observed context (survives 4000-char truncation) so `extract_mechanism_signals()` can map them to literature. Blob guard: reports with unresolved blob tokens (`@scai-blob:...`) are skipped during mechanism signal extraction.
  - **Report analysis log (`backend/services/report/analysis_log.py`)**: `build_round_summaries()` now includes per-round `mechanism_programs` (label, cell_types, observations, genes, strength, specificity, bulk_support, missing), bounded to top 3 per round.
  - **Report cross-modal/mechanism section (`backend/services/report/planning.py`)**: `insert_cross_modal_section()` falls back to title "Data-Driven Mechanism Candidates" and includes mechanism program data when no cross-modal candidates exist. `_generate_section_from_existing_figures()` injects mechanism programs into cross-modal context. Bounded to most recent 3 rounds.
  - **17 new test files**: `test_detailed_report_mechanism.py`, `test_direction_candidates_mechanism_prompt.py`, `test_final_summary_mechanism_prompt.py`, `test_integrated_report_mechanism_prompt.py`, `test_literature_mechanism_blob_guard.py`, `test_literature_observed_context_mechanism.py`, `test_mechanism_gate_source.py`, `test_mechanism_handler_return.py`, `test_mechanism_plumbing_source.py`, `test_mechanism_program_schema.py`, `test_mechanism_program_validation.py`, `test_mechanism_tool_schema.py`, `test_stage2_instruction_mechanism.py`, `test_supervisor_report_prompt_mechanism.py`. Plus `test_to_api_dict_coverage.py` updated.

- `0.30.1` — Cross-modal mechanism audit & literature UpstreamExhaustedError propagation (2026-06-30):
  - **Cross-modal mechanism synthesis (`backend/workflow/synthesis.py`)**: New `_build_literature_audit_context()` and `_build_biochemistry_raw_context()` build bounded multi-modal evidence surfaces for the PI. Cross-modal audit runs during innovation-round synthesis (`_synthesize_round`): PI LLM call (`pi_agent.build_cross_modal_synthesis()`) produces a `CrossModalSynthesis` with evidence matrix and mechanism candidates graded by confidence, consistency, and report recommendation. Round summary includes `cross_modal_candidates` for frontend display.
  - **PI cross-modal builder (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`)**: `build_cross_modal_synthesis()` takes raw agent result dicts, bounded literature context, and raw biochemistry measurements — produces a cross-modal evidence matrix and honestly-graded mechanism candidates. `_parse_cross_modal_response()` recovers gracefully from unparseable LLM responses. `synthesize_round()` accepts `cross_modal_context` parameter. `generate_final_summary()` includes cross-modal mechanism candidates with `do_not_emphasize` filter — falls back to lightweight literature summary when no candidates exist.
  - **Literature mechanism signal extraction (`backend/agents/literature_agent.py`)**: `extract_mechanism_signals()` is a generic, mechanism-agnostic extraction of biological mechanisms from retrieved literature that co-occur with observed experimental data. Non-critical: returns `None` on any failure. `UpstreamExhaustedError` re-raise at both `process()` and PubMed-fallback layers prevents silent degradation to `quick_search` — the workflow LLM-failure HITL gate now correctly propagates upstream exhaustion instead of hiding it behind a low-quality fallback result.
  - **Workflow agent persistence (`backend/workflow/agents.py`)**: `_persist_literature_mechanism_signals()` extracts mechanism signals from the current round's literature reports and persists them onto `LiteratureReport.mechanism_signals`. Guards against no-data/skipped rounds. `_build_observed_context_for_literature()` builds current-round agent results or fallback prior-round findings. Signals are round-level aggregates attached to every current-round report for idempotent reload.
  - **New Pydantic models (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: `LiteratureMechanismSignal` (mechanism, supporting_pmids, evidence_type, relevance, observed_data_links, limitations), `CrossModalEvidenceItem` (source, evidence, strength, limitation), `CrossModalMechanismCandidate` (theme, mechanism_statement, supporting_evidence, evidence_consistency, confidence, novelty, relation_to_current_direction, missing_evidence, report_recommendation), `CrossModalSynthesis` (matrix_markdown, candidates, excluded_or_low_support_themes). `LiteratureReport` gains `mechanism_signals`. `RoundResult` gains `cross_modal_synthesis`. `AnalysisState._serialize_round_results()` includes simplified candidate summary.
  - **Report cross-modal appendix (`backend/services/report/generator.py`)**: `build_cross_modal_appendix()` assembles per-round full cross-modal synthesis detail (including `excluded_or_low_support_themes`) for the report JSON appendix.
  - **Report cross-modal section insertion (`backend/services/report/planning.py`)**: `insert_cross_modal_section()` places a "Cross-Modal Mechanism Candidates" section before the first closing section (conclusion/references/limitations). Only `report_recommendation != "do_not_emphasize"` candidates are included. `_generate_section_from_existing_figures()` injects cross-modal context into the per-section generation prompt. `REPORT_RECO_ORDER` and `CLOSING_SECTION_MARKERS` constants support deterministic placement.
  - **Report analysis log shared helper (`backend/services/report/analysis_log.py`)**: `build_round_summaries()` extracted as a shared function returning per-round dicts with simplified `cross_modal_candidates`, reused by both `AnalysisLogMixin.build_analysis_log()` and `planning.py`.
  - **Frontend cross-modal types (`frontend/src/types.ts`)**: New `CrossModalCandidate` interface with `theme`, `mechanism_statement`, `confidence`, `relation_to_current_direction`, `report_recommendation` fields. `RoundSummary` gains `cross_modal_candidates`.
  - **Frontend store & API (`frontend/src/stores/appStore.ts`, `frontend/src/hooks/useApi.ts`, `frontend/src/utils/transformLoadResponse.ts`)**: Cross-modal candidate support — `RoundSummary` in store carries candidates; transform load maps snake_case `cross_modal_candidates` to camelCase.
  - **Frontend ResultsPanel display (`frontend/src/components/ResultsPanel.tsx`)**: New cross-modal mechanism card layout — theme/mechanism statement with confidence badge, evidence consistency, relation badge, recommendation tag, evidence sources list (icon+color per modality), evidence gaps section. Collapsible per-candidate.
  - **Frontend i18n (`frontend/src/i18n/translations.ts`)**: New `crossModal` translation block with keys for `title`, `theme`, `mechanism`, `confidence`, `consistency`, `relation`, `evidence`, `evidenceGaps`, `recommendation`.
  - **13 new test files**: `test_cross_modal_fixture_quality.py`, `test_cross_modal_models.py`, `test_cross_modal_roundtrip.py`, `test_cross_modal_serialization.py`, `test_pi_synthesis_cross_modal.py`, `test_build_cross_modal_synthesis.py`, `test_final_summary_cross_modal.py`, `test_synthesize_round_cross_modal.py`, `test_report_appendix_cross_modal.py`, `test_report_plan_cross_modal_section.py`, `test_analysis_log_cross_modal.py`, `test_literature_mechanism_extraction.py`, `test_literature_node_mechanism_persist.py`, `test_literature_upstream_exhausted_propagates.py`. Frontend: `crossModal.test.ts`.

**0.29.x — (2026-06-24 → 2026-06-29)**

- `0.29.10` — Plan JSON parse failure fallback: four-layer defense (2026-06-29):
  - **Layer 1 — json_repair recovery (`backend/utils/formatting.py`)**: `safe_parse_json` now tries `json_repair` on the bracket-extracted segment, then the full response, after bracket extraction fails due to unbalanced quotes. Prevents a single stray `"` in LLM output from producing a 0-step plan. Graceful degradation when `json_repair` is unavailable (deferring to Layers 2-4).
  - **Layer 2A/2B — Parse-failure retry with cache-busting (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: `generate_analysis_plan` and `revise_plan_incrementally` detect parse errors or empty steps and retry once with a UUID cache-busting token (`[retry:<hex>]`) and explicit JSON-format instructions. The `f"..."` prefix on the UUID line is critical for correctness — a missing `f` produces an identical retry prompt and the server-side cache returns the same malformed response.
  - **Layer 2.5 — Never discard existing steps on revision (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: When `revise_plan_incrementally` produces 0 steps (parse failure or LLM returned empty), returns a new `AnalysisPlan` derived from `current_plan` with a `revision_reason` explaining the retention. `depends_on=list(s.depends_on)` avoids shared mutable list references.
  - **Layer 4 — MAX_PLANNING_ROUNDS fallback to history (`backend/workflow/planning.py`)**: When max rounds are exhausted, prefers `existing_plan` only if it has steps and was not PI-rejected. Otherwise scans `dialogue_history` in reverse for the last valid non-empty plan using `AnalysisPlan.model_validate`. Manual fallback preserves `id`, `revision_of`, `revision_reason`; `AnalysisPlan.id` default_factory handles missing `id`. Skips PI-rejected history entries. Last resort calls `generate_final_plan_from_dialogue` for PI unilateral generation.
  - **Layer 5 — User review gate rejects empty plans (`backend/workflow/planning.py`)**: `_user_review_gate` now checks `not plan or not plan.steps`, logging at `logger.error` level (all prior layers failed), then sets `should_continue=False` and routes to final report without sending a `plan_review` message.
  - **New test files**: `test_safe_parse_json_unbalanced_quote.py` (3 tests: repair, repair-with-wrapping-text, no-repair fallback), `test_bioinformatics_plan_parse_retry.py` (5 tests: retry prompt uniqueness, generate retry on empty steps, retry fallback, revise preserves current plan, revise empty steps preserves), `test_planning_dialogue_llm_failure.py` (5 new tests: history picks last valid, rejected existing plan uses history, skipping rejected history, no-valid-history calls PI final, user review gate rejects empty).
  - **Design spec**: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-29-plan-parse-fallback-design.md` documents the full analysis with session 56145c54 timeline, defense-in-depth trace, and all 16 unit test cases.
  - **Project context files**: Added `AGENTS.md` and `CLAUDE.md` for agentic workers (OpenCode/Claude).

- `0.29.9` — ExplorationReport.from_llm_dict type-tolerant parser, direction selection gate reliability fix (2026-06-28):
  - **Type-tolerant ExplorationReport construction (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: New `ExplorationReport.from_llm_dict()` classmethod validates each field individually via `TypeAdapter`, dropping type-mismatched values (e.g. a string for `Dict[str, Any]` `direction_selection_summary`) instead of raising a `ValidationError`. A dropped field falls back to its Pydantic default, preventing a single malformed LLM output from aborting Report-A generation and silently skipping the exploration→innovation transition and direction-selection gate.
  - **Direction selection gate preservation (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`)**: `score_directions_with_evidence` switched from `ExplorationReport(**valid_fields)` to `ExplorationReport.from_llm_dict(data)`, ensuring that a type-mismatched LLM field no longer crashes Report-A generation.
  - **Regression tests (`backend/tests/test_direction_evidence_models.py`)**: Four tests cover string-in-dict-field, valid-field-preservation, list-getting-string, and unknown-field-ignored scenarios.

- `0.29.8` — Reasoning model truncation guard, auto-download marker DB HITL, sandbox FD passthrough, max_tokens bump for reasoning models (2026-06-28):
  - **Reasoning model truncation guard (`backend/utils/llm_client.py`)**: New `_response_length_truncated()` and `_answer_content_empty()` static methods detect when a reasoning model exhausts its `max_tokens` budget on `reasoning_content`. On `finish_reason="length"` with empty answer content, retries once with `max_tokens` bumped to `min(max(max_tokens * 4, _TRUNCATION_RETRY_FLOOR=4096), _TRUNCATION_RETRY_CEILING=32768)`. `_truncation_retried` sentinel prevents infinite retry. Prevents silent empty answers from reasoning models without affecting non-reasoning models.
  - **max_tokens bump for reasoning models (`backend/agents/supervisor_agent.py`, `technique_agent.py`, `pi_agent.py`)**: Supervisor checkpoint/code-review calls bumped from 512/1024 to 4096; Technique agent summarization calls from 1024 to 4096; PI direction-figure-name call from 512 to 4096. Figure review callback (`callbacks.py`) bumped from 1024 to 4096.
  - **Sandbox FD passthrough (`backend/utils/sandbox.py`)**: Integer file descriptors (used by multiprocessing/dask pipes in GRNBoost2) now bypass `_sandboxed_open` write-access checks — they are already-open resources not subject to path-based permissions.
  - **Marker DB missing HITL auto-download (`backend/workflow/core.py`)**: First attempt silently downloads without user dialog. Only on failure does the HITL popup appear, with a retry loop that re-prompts on repeated download failures. Error message: "Automatic download failed" instead of "TSV not found".
  - **Marker DB tests rewritten (`backend/tests/test_marker_db_missing_hitl.py`)**: 3 tests cover auto-download-success (no dialog), auto-fail+retry (dialog→success), auto-fail+terminate.
  - **bar/barh positional arg guidance (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: Rule 9 warns against passing width/height as both positional arg and keyword in `ax.bar()`/`ax.barh()`.
  - **Frontend i18n (`frontend/src/i18n/translations.ts`)**: Marker DB dialog title/desc/button changed from "needed"/"Fetch" to "download failed"/"Retry download" in both Chinese and English.

- `0.29.7` — Figure deduplication fix: auto-save wrapper refactored to save/display only newly-created figures, safe plt.close through no-op stubs, baseline plt.close/show captured before monkey-patch (2026-06-28):
  - **`_build_exec_autosave_wrapper()` extracted (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: Inline figure auto-save code moved into reusable factory function. Snapshot of `plt.get_fignums()` taken before user code execution; only figures whose numbers are NOT in the pre-existing set are saved/displayed (`_pre_fignums` diff filter). Prevents re-dumping stale figures accumulated in the kernel — the root cause of the same image appearing 100+ times in reports.
  - **Safe `plt.close` through no-op stubs (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: `_TPL_SAFE_CLOSE_ALL` now uses `_original_plt_close` baseline (captured before monkey-patches) instead of `plt.close`, which may be a SCAI no-op stub left by interrupted cells. Avoids silent no-op close where figures only accumulate.
  - **Baseline close/show capture (`backend/utils/sandbox.py`)**: `_original_plt_close` and `_original_plt_show` captured before the Combined Monkey-patch installs stubs over them, placed before `<WORKSPACE_DIR>` code to avoid NameError dead-code issue.
  - **3 new regression tests (`backend/tests/test_figure_dedup.py`)**: `test_safe_close_all_closes_through_noop_stub` (close works through no-op), `test_exec_wrapper_saves_only_new_figures` (3 runs × 1 fig = 3 files, not 6), `test_exec_wrapper_filters_by_pre_fignums` (template contains diff filter).

- `0.29.6` — Direction exploration optimization: enhanced evidence structure (support/contradiction/prior-art levels, novelty status), minimum-attempt per-candidate budget with tier-3 broad fallback queries, active challenge/prior-art query generation, PI scoring guardrails (core-observation validity, literature timeout awareness, premise conflict enforcement), soft retry for critical direction fields, enhanced frontend candidate/evidence display with backward compatibility (2026-06-27):
  - **Enhanced direction evidence structure (`backend/agents/literature_agent.py`)**: Direction literature evidence now carries `support_level` (strong/moderate/weak/none/unknown), `contradiction_level`, `prior_art_level` (high/medium/low/unknown), `novelty_status` (genuine_novelty/known_area/unknown_due_to_timeout/unknown_due_to_failure/unknown), `similar_published_directions`, `knowledge_gap_summary`, `fallback_tier`, and `scheduler_limitation`. `_finalize_seeded_direction_result()` produces all enhanced fields from base evidence. `_partition_evidence()` splits evidence by support type.
  - **Active challenge/contradiction and prior-art queries (`backend/agents/literature_agent.py`)**: `_build_active_challenge_query()` generates adversarial PubMed queries with contradiction/keywords (contradict, negative, alternative, contrary). `_build_prior_art_query()` generates prior-art queries scoped to organism+tissue. Both integrated into `_direction_queries()` alongside existing builders. `_build_tier3_broad_query()` produces stripped no-field-tag fallback queries for degraded evidence collection.
  - **Minimum attempt budget per candidate (`backend/agents/literature_agent.py`)**: `_minimum_attempt_queries()` generates at least one support or challenge query per candidate, falling back to tier-3 broad support/challenge pair. `_evaluate_minimum_attempt_candidate()` calls `_execute_query_batch` with per-query timeout from `MIN_ATTEMPT_QUERY_TIMEOUT` (10s). When standard evaluation returns no queries or fails, the minimum-attempt result is used with `scheduler_limitation` explaining why full evaluation was unavailable.
  - **Candidate budget fairness (`evaluate_direction_novelty_candidates()`)**: New `max_full_evidence_candidates` parameter (default 4) caps the number of candidates receiving full support/challenge/prior-art evaluation. Candidates beyond the cap receive minimum-attempt evaluation with `scheduler_limitation="full_evaluation_skipped_by_candidate_cap"`. Tests confirm slow first candidates do not starve later ones of their minimum attempt.
  - **Novelty status resolution (`_resolve_novelty_status()`)**: Distinguishes genuine_novelty (evidence found but no direct prior work) from known_area (direct prior work exists), unknown_due_to_timeout (deadline expired before any evidence) and unknown_due_to_failure (exception before any evidence). Timed-out candidates still record attempted queries and fallback_tier.
  - **PI scoring guardrails (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`)**: `_apply_direction_guardrails()` enforces: (1) `core_observation` must cite a specific data element or recommendation downgrades to `needs_more_data` via `_candidate_has_specific_core_observation()`; (2) prioritize recommendation is downgraded when data_reliability=low, artifact_risk=high, or data_sufficiency=insufficient; (3) literature novelty set to `unknown` when novelty_status is timeout/failure; (4) premise-conflict candidates get `contradicts_premise` risk flag. `_critical_direction_fields_missing()` identifies absent critical fields for soft retry.
  - **PI soft retry for critical fields (`_soft_retry_direction_candidate()`)**: Top-priority candidates with missing critical fields (core_observation, alternative_hypotheses, artifact_risks, contradictory_or_missing_evidence, validation_plan, recommendation) receive one LLM retry with round context. On failure, `critical_field_retry_failed` flag is appended instead of blocking the report.
  - **PI enhanced candidate generation prompts**: `generate_raw_direction_candidates()` prompt now requires `core_observation`, `main_hypothesis`, `alternative_hypotheses` (at least 2), `artifact_risks`, `suggested_validation_analysis`, `suggested_wet_lab_validation`, and `decision_criteria`. `score_directions_with_evidence()` prompt extended with `biological_plausibility`, `validation_feasibility`, `artifact_risk`, `recommendation`, `pi_review_summary`, `contradictory_or_missing_evidence`, `validation_plan`, `contradicts_stated_premise` — each with scoring rubric definitions and guardrail rules.
  - **Direction evidence models extended (`backend/models/schemas.py`)**: `DirectionLiteratureEvidence` gains 7 new optional fields: `support_level`, `contradiction_level`, `prior_art_level`, `novelty_status`, `similar_published_directions`, `knowledge_gap_summary`, `fallback_tier`, `scheduler_limitation`. `DirectionSearchQuery.category` extended with `challenge_contradiction`, `prior_art`, `tier3_support`, `tier3_challenge`.
  - **Fallback direction evidence enhanced (`backend/utils/direction_evidence.py`)**: `build_fallback_direction_evidence()` now populates `novelty_status=unknown_due_to_failure`, `support_level=unknown`, `contradiction_level=unknown`, `prior_art_level=unknown`, `fallback_tier=failed`, `knowledge_gap_summary=<reason>`.
  - **Protocol probe sleep removed (`backend/utils/llm_client.py`)**: Removed `asyncio.sleep(1.0)` between inter-protocol probes — PROBE_RETRY_DELAYS already handles transient backoff, and protocol switches should be immediate.
  - **Settings provider sealed secret contract (`backend/utils/settings_provider.py`)**: Added forward-reference comment linking `MASKED_SECRET_VALUE` to `frontend/src/utils/literatureConfig.ts` to ensure both sides stay in sync.
  - **Test stability fix (`test_direction_literature_triage.py`)**: Direction timeout-before-query test updated to expect tier-3 fallback queries when standard queries are skipped by expired deadline — minimum-attempt path ensures timed-out candidates still record attempted queries.
  - **Frontend enhanced evidence display (`frontend/src/components/analysis/DirectionEvidence.tsx`)**: Shows support_level, contradiction_level, prior_art_level, novelty_status, fallback_tier badges in detail panel. `knowledge_gap_summary` displayed in bordered box. `scheduler_limitation` shown as amber warning. Up to 5 `similar_published_directions` listed under evidence details.
  - **Frontend enhanced candidate display (`frontend/src/components/ExplorationReportDialog.tsx`)**: `DirectionCandidate` interface extended with `core_observation`, `main_hypothesis`, `alternative_hypotheses`, `artifact_risks`, `supporting_data`, `contradictory_or_missing_evidence`, `contradicts_stated_premise`, `validation_plan`, `biological_plausibility`, `validation_feasibility`, `artifact_risk`, `recommendation`, `pi_review_summary`. Summary badges: recommendation (Prioritize/Secondary/Needs more data/Avoid), biological_plausibility, artifact_risk, premise conflict warning. Expanded details: core observation, main hypothesis, supporting data, alternative hypotheses, artifact risks, contradictory/missing evidence, validation plan, PI review. `RecommendationBadge` component. `DetailList` component. Amber warning for `needs_more_data` or `artifact_risk=high`. Visual de-emphasis for low-confidence recommendations.
  - **Frontend evidence interface extended (`frontend/src/components/ExplorationReportDialog.tsx`)**: `DirectionLiteratureEvidence` gains `support_level`, `contradiction_level`, `prior_art_level`, `novelty_status`, `similar_published_directions`, `knowledge_gap_summary`, `fallback_tier`, `scheduler_limitation` fields.
  - **15 new/updated test cases**: `test_direction_literature_triage.py` (3 new: challenge+prior-art query generation, tier-3 broad query format, slow first candidate does not starve later candidates, timed-out zero-evidence records novelty_status and fallback), `test_direction_evidence_models.py` (2 new: enhanced evidence round-trip, new query categories), `test_fallback_direction_evidence.py` (1 new: enhanced failure metadata), `test_pi_score_field_contract.py` (3 new: specific core_observation detection, guardrail downgrades, literature timeout conservatism, critical fields detection), `ExplorationReportDialog.test.tsx` (2 new: enhanced fields+premise conflict, enhanced literature evidence metadata).

- `0.29.5` — Direction evidence deadline enforcement: per-direction monotonic deadline preserves partially-collected evidence, report visual distinction between timeout-without-evidence and partial-with-evidence (2026-06-26):
  - **Per-direction deadline enforcement (`backend/agents/literature_agent.py`)**: `evaluate_direction_novelty_candidates()` replaced the single `asyncio.wait_for` wrapping the entire candidate with a `time.monotonic()` deadline that propagates through `_evaluate_single_candidate()` → `_run_query_batches()` → `_execute_query_batch()`. Each sub-step (cache triage, PubMed search, LLM triage) computes a `remaining_timeout()` from the deadline and caps its own `asyncio.wait_for`. When the deadline expires mid-batch, the current query is interrupted but evidence collected before the deadline is preserved in the result. `_evaluate_single_candidate()` checks the deadline before running standard queries, deep queries, and each batch iteration. Empty-result timeouts (deadline expires before any step succeeds) are marked as `timed_out=True` with zero counts, while partial-collection timeouts retain the evidence gathered so far.
  - **Report timed-out badge (`backend/services/report/variants.py`)**: `_status_badge()` renders `"Timed out"` (badge-na, gray) when `status=partial && timed_out && total_evidence==0`, reserving `"Partial"` (badge-medium, amber) for partial status with retained references. Warning messages similarly distinguish "timed out before references could be retained" from "only partially completed; listed references are partial."
  - **Frontend visual distinction (`frontend/src/components/analysis/DirectionEvidence.tsx`)**: New gray "Search timed out" badge for timed-out zero-evidence directions; amber "Partial evidence" badge preserved for directions with retained references. Detail panel shows contextual messages: "timed out before references could be retained" (gray) vs "references below are partial" (amber). Existing "Literature verification failed" message refined to "failed or was skipped."
  - **Frontend type expansion (`frontend/src/components/ExplorationReportDialog.tsx`)**: `DirectionLiteratureEvidence` interface extended with `timed_out` and `timeout_seconds` fields.
  - **Frontend cleanup (`frontend/src/components/AnnotationConfirmationDialog.tsx`)**: Removed unused `validationStatus` prop.
  - **5 new test cases**: 3 backend async tests (`test_direction_literature_triage.py` — deadline preservation, early-expiry zero-counts, in-flight budget limiting), 2 backend HTML tests (`test_exploration_report_direction_evidence_html.py` — timed-out zero-ref badge, partial-with-refs badge), 2 frontend Vitest tests (`ExplorationReportDialog.test.tsx` — search timed out display, partial evidence with retained PMID).

- `0.29.4` — Annotation validation round: PI-planned restricted bioinformatics checks + PI revision before user annotation confirmation (2026-06-26):
  - **Annotation validation round (`backend/workflow/annotation.py`, `annotation_validation.py`)**: New `_run_annotation_validation_round()` orchestration that runs between PI literature annotation and user confirmation. Gated by trigger conditions (low/medium confidence, uncertainty keywords, risk triage buckets). PI generates biological validation questions (`generate_annotation_validation_questions()`), bioinformatics agent executes restricted code checks on a `.copy()` of the main AnnData object with invariant guards (`signatures_match()`), and PI revises annotations based on evidence (`revise_annotations_after_validation()`). Idempotent via deterministic `compute_validation_attempt_id()` for snapshot-restore/WS-reconnect safety. Post-supplementary analysis re-validates with `force=True`.
  - **Main-object invariant protection (`signatures_match()`)**: Compares 12-key signature before/after each validation code iteration. Detects in-place mutations of `.X`, `.obs`, `.layers`, cluster counts, or content fingerprints. Aborts on first mutation with `"failed"` status. Order-insensitive for set-valued keys (`obs_columns`, `layers`). Missing `after` signature is always unsafe.
  - **Validation result sanitization (`sanitize_validation_result()`)**: Caps summary at 4000 chars, artifacts at 30, warnings at 30, cluster_results at 200. Enforces text-first rule — every figure artifact must carry a non-empty `summary` or status downgrades to `partial`.
  - **PI planning & revision agents (`backend/agents/pi_agent.py`)**: `generate_annotation_validation_questions()` produces JSON tasks with cluster_ids, biological questions, suggested checks, decision rules. `revise_annotations_after_validation()` merges validation evidence into annotations, preserving untouched fields.
  - **Restricted bioinformatics executor (`backend/agents/bioinformatics_agent.py`)**: `validate_annotations_with_plan()` — sandbox code generation on `adata.copy()` with strict denylist (no DEG/pathway/communication/trajectory/velocity/clustering/filtering/normalization) and per-iteration invariant check. Artifacts saved under absolute `{workspace}/annotation_validation/` path. `capture_main_object_signature()` / `introspect_validation_metadata()` helpers.
  - **Reclustering recommendations**: Clusters flagged as `requires_reclustering` by validation evidence carry `reclustering_reason` and `suggested_reclustering_scope` through `ClusterAnnotation` model and frontend UI.
  - **AnnotationConfirmationState extended**: 6 new optional validation fields (`validation_attempt_id`, `validation_status`, `validation_plan`, `validation_result`, `validation_summary`, `validation_text_summary`, `reclustering_recommendations`). `ClusterAnnotation` gains `requires_reclustering`, `reclustering_reason`, `suggested_reclustering_scope`.
  - **Config gate (`backend/config/_config.py`)**: `enable_annotation_validation_round` (default True), `annotation_validation_timeout_seconds` (180), `annotation_validation_max_iterations` (5, hard-capped at 8). All validated via Pydantic field constraints.
  - **Frontend validation UI (`frontend/src/components/AnnotationConfirmationDialog.tsx`)**: Blue validation evidence banner above annotations showing summary, text verdicts, artifact links (`/api/file?path=`), and amber reclustering recommendations.
  - **Artifact path resolver (`resolve_artifact_path()`)**: Reroutes stray absolute paths (e.g. repo paths containing `/workspace/`) to session-rooted absolute paths, ensuring `/api/file` ownership validation passes.
